===== M0013 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 835-877
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
835 |   @Override
836 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
837 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
838 |       return true;
839 |     }
840 | 
841 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
842 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
843 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
844 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
845 |         return true;
846 |       }
847 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
848 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
849 |         return false;
850 |       }
851 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
852 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
853 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
854 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
855 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
856 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
857 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
858 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
859 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
860 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
861 | 
862 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
863 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
864 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
865 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
866 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
867 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
868 | 
869 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
870 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
871 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
872 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
873 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
874 |     }
875 | 
876 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
877 |   }

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 452-481
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
452 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
453 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
454 |     int type = n.getType();
455 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
456 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
457 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
458 |     }
459 | 
460 |     curNode = n;
461 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
462 | 
463 |     switch (type) {
464 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
465 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
466 |         break;
467 | 
468 |       default:
469 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
470 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
471 |           // would no longer point to the true next
472 |           Node next = child.getNext();
473 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
474 |           child = next;
475 |         }
476 |         break;
477 |     }
478 | 
479 |     curNode = n;
480 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
481 |   }

/**
     * Builds a method call based on the the given method name,
     * argument and history.
     *
     * @param methodName Method to call.
     * @param argumentNode Method argument.
     * @param lineno line number in original source.
     * @param charno character offset in original line.
     */

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

163 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
164 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
165 |                                    String sourceString,
166 |                                    Config config,
167 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
168 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 601-659
Documentation:

Method code:
601 |     @Override
602 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
603 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
604 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
605 |       if (name == null) {
606 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
607 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
608 |           errorReporter.error(
609 |             "unnamed function statement",
610 |             sourceName,
611 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
612 |         }
613 |         name = new Name();
614 |         name.setIdentifier("");
615 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
616 |       }
617 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
618 |       Node newName = transform(name);
619 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
620 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
621 |         // declaration.
622 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
623 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
624 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
625 |         // same line as the function keyword.
626 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
627 |             functionNode.getLp();
628 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
629 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
630 |       }
631 | 
632 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
633 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
634 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
635 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
636 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
637 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
638 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
639 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
640 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
641 |       if (fnName != null) {
642 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
643 |       } else {
644 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
645 |       }
646 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
647 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
648 | 
649 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
650 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
651 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
652 |       }
653 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
654 | 
655 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
656 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
657 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
658 |      return node;
659 |     }

===== M0071 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2513-2523
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2513 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2514 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2515 |     int len = s.length();
2516 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2517 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2518 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2519 |         return false;
2520 |       }
2521 |     }
2522 |     return true;
2523 |   }

===== M0072 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processInfixExpression(InfixExpression)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 672-688
Documentation:

Method code:
672 |     @Override
673 |     Node processInfixExpression(InfixExpression exprNode) {
674 |       Node n =  newNode(
675 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
676 |           transform(exprNode.getLeft()),
677 |           transform(exprNode.getRight()));
678 |       // Set the line number here so we can fine-tune it in ways transform
679 |       // doesn't do.
680 |       n.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
681 |       // Position in new ASTNode is to start of expression, but old-fashioned
682 |       // line numbers from Node reference the operator token.  Add the offset
683 |       // to the operator to get the correct character number.
684 |       n.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
685 |           exprNode.getOperatorPosition()));
686 |       maybeSetLengthFrom(n, exprNode);
687 |       return n;
688 |     }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 774-788
Documentation:

Method code:
774 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
775 |     boolean valid = false;
776 |     if (i == 0) {
777 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
778 |     } else if (i == 1) {
779 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
780 |     } else {
781 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
782 |     }
783 |     if (!valid) {
784 |       violation(
785 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
786 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
787 |     }
788 |   }

Method code:
1757 |   static boolean isStatementParent(Node parent) {
1758 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1759 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1760 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1761 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1762 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1763 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1764 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1765 |       case Token.LABEL:
1766 |         return true;
1767 |       default:
1768 |         return false;
1769 |     }
1770 |   }

481 |     @Override
482 |     public Node reduce(Node node) {
483 |       if (!isReduceableFunctionExpression(node)) {
484 |         return node;
485 |       }
486 | 
487 |       Node propName = getSetPropertyName(node);
488 |       if (propName != null) {

499 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.replaceChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 796-817
Documentation:
/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
796 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
797 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
798 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
799 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
800 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
801 | 
802 |     // Copy over important information.
803 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
804 | 
805 |     newChild.next = child.next;
806 |     newChild.parent = this;
807 |     if (child == first) {
808 |         first = newChild;
809 |     } else {
810 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
811 |         prev.next = newChild;
812 |     }
813 |     if (child == last)
814 |         last = newChild;
815 |     child.next = null;
816 |     child.parent = null;
817 |   }

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 86-735
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(
100 |           childCount == 2,
101 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
102 |           opstr, childCount);
103 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
104 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
105 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
106 | 
107 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
108 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
109 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
110 | 
111 |       // Handle associativity.
112 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
113 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
114 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
115 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
118 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
119 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
120 |       } else {
121 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
122 |       }
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
127 | 
128 |     switch (type) {
129 |       case Token.TRY: {
130 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
131 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
132 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
133 | 
134 |         add("try");
135 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 | 
137 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
138 |         // isn't a catch block
139 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
140 |         if (catchblock != null) {
141 |           add(catchblock);
142 |         }
143 | 
144 |         if (childCount == 3) {
145 |           add("finally");
146 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
147 |         }
148 |         break;
149 |       }
150 | 
151 |       case Token.CATCH:
152 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
153 |         add("catch(");
154 |         add(first);
155 |         add(")");
156 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
157 |         break;
158 | 
159 |       case Token.THROW:
160 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
161 |         add("throw");
162 |         add(first);
163 | 
164 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
165 |         // parse this.
166 |         cc.endStatement(true);
167 |         break;
168 | 
169 |       case Token.RETURN:
170 |         add("return");
171 |         if (childCount == 1) {
172 |           add(first);
173 |         } else {
174 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
175 |         }
176 |         cc.endStatement();
177 |         break;
178 | 
179 |       case Token.VAR:
180 |         if (first != null) {
181 |           add("var ");
182 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 | 
186 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
187 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
188 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
189 |         break;
190 | 
191 |       case Token.NAME:
192 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |         } else {
195 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
196 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
197 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
198 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
199 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
200 |           } else {
201 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
202 |             // precedence.
203 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
204 |           }
205 |         }
206 |         break;
207 | 
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |         add("[");
210 |         addArrayList(first);
211 |         add("]");
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.LP:
215 |         add("(");
216 |         addList(first);
217 |         add(")");
218 |         break;
219 | 
220 |       case Token.COMMA:
221 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
222 |         addList(first, false, context);
223 |         break;
224 | 
225 |       case Token.NUMBER:
226 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
227 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
228 |         break;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
231 |       case Token.VOID:
232 |       case Token.NOT:
233 |       case Token.BITNOT:
234 |       case Token.POS: {
235 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
237 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
238 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
239 |         break;
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       case Token.NEG: {
243 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
244 | 
245 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
246 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
247 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
248 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
249 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
250 |         } else {
251 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
252 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
253 |         }
254 | 
255 |         break;
256 |       }
257 | 
258 |       case Token.HOOK: {
259 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
260 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
261 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
262 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
263 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
264 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
265 |         addExpr(last, 1);
266 |         break;
267 |       }
268 | 
269 |       case Token.REGEXP:
270 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
271 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
272 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
273 |         }
274 | 
275 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
276 | 
277 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
278 |         if (childCount == 2) {
279 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
280 |         } else {
281 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
282 |           add(regexp);
283 |         }
284 |         break;
285 | 
286 |       case Token.GET_REF:
287 |         add(first);
288 |         break;
289 | 
290 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
292 |         add(first);
293 |         add(".");
294 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
295 |         break;
296 | 
297 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
298 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
299 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
300 |         }
301 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
302 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
303 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
304 |           add("(");
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         add("function");
308 |         add(first);
309 | 
310 |         add(first.getNext());
311 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
312 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
313 | 
314 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
315 |           add(")");
316 |         }
317 |         break;
318 | 
319 |       case Token.GET:
320 |       case Token.SET:
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
323 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
324 | 
325 |         // Get methods are unnamed
326 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
327 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
328 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
329 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
330 |           add("get ");
331 |         } else {
332 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
333 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
334 |           add("set ");
335 |         }
336 | 
337 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
338 |         String name = n.getString();
339 |         Node fn = first;
340 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
341 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
342 | 
343 |         // Add the property name.
344 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
345 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
346 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
347 |             // unicode escaped.
348 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
349 |           add(name);
350 |         } else {
351 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
352 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
353 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
354 |             cc.addNumber(d);
355 |           } else {
356 |             addJsString(n.getString());
357 |           }
358 |         }
359 | 
360 |         add(parameters);
361 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
362 |         break;
363 | 
364 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
365 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
366 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
367 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
368 |         }
369 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
370 |         if (preserveBlock) {
371 |           cc.beginBlock();
372 |         }
373 | 
374 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
375 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
376 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
377 |                 !preserveBlock &&
378 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
379 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
380 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
381 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
382 | 
383 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
384 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
385 |             cc.endStatement();
386 |           }
387 | 
388 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
389 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
390 |           }
391 | 
392 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
393 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
394 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
395 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
396 |           }
397 |         }
398 |         if (preserveBlock) {
399 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
400 |         }
401 |         break;
402 |       }
403 | 
404 |       case Token.FOR:
405 |         if (childCount == 4) {
406 |           add("for(");
407 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
408 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
409 |           } else {
410 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
411 |           }
412 |           add(";");
413 |           add(first.getNext());
414 |           add(";");
415 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
416 |           add(")");
417 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
418 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
419 |         } else {
420 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
421 |           add("for(");
422 |           add(first);
423 |           add("in");
424 |           add(first.getNext());
425 |           add(")");
426 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
427 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
428 |         }
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.DO:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("do");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(last);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         cc.endStatement();
439 |         break;
440 | 
441 |       case Token.WHILE:
442 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(first);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
447 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
448 |         break;
449 | 
450 |       case Token.EMPTY:
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
452 |         break;
453 | 
454 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
455 |         Preconditions.checkState(
456 |             childCount == 2,
457 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
458 |         Preconditions.checkState(
459 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
460 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
461 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
462 |         if (needsParens) {
463 |           add("(");
464 |         }
465 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
466 |         if (needsParens) {
467 |           add(")");
468 |         }
469 |         add(".");
470 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
471 |         break;
472 |       }
473 | 
474 |       case Token.GETELEM:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(
476 |             childCount == 2,
477 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
478 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
479 |         add("[");
480 |         add(first.getNext());
481 |         add("]");
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.WITH:
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
486 |         add("with(");
487 |         add(first);
488 |         add(")");
489 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
490 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.INC:
494 |       case Token.DEC: {
495 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
496 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
497 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
498 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
499 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
500 |         if (postProp != 0) {
501 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
502 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
503 |         } else {
504 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
505 |           add(first);
506 |         }
507 |         break;
508 |       }
509 | 
510 |       case Token.CALL:
511 |         // We have two special cases here:
512 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
513 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
515 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
516 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
517 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
518 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
519 |         // that must be preserved.
520 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
521 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
522 |           add("(0,");
523 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
524 |           add(")");
525 |         } else {
526 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
527 |         }
528 |         add("(");
529 |         addList(first.getNext());
530 |         add(")");
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.IF:
534 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
535 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
536 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
537 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
538 |           cc.beginBlock();
539 |         }
540 | 
541 |         add("if(");
542 |         add(first);
543 |         add(")");
544 | 
545 |         if (hasElse) {
546 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
547 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
548 |           add("else");
549 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
550 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
551 |         } else {
552 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
553 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
554 |         }
555 | 
556 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
557 |           cc.endBlock();
558 |         }
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.NULL:
562 |       case Token.THIS:
563 |       case Token.FALSE:
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
602 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
603 | 
604 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
605 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
606 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
607 |         cc.endStatement();
608 |         break;
609 | 
610 |       case Token.NEW:
611 |         add("new ");
612 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
613 | 
614 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
615 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
616 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
617 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 956-983
Documentation:

Method code:
956 |   String jsString(String s) {
957 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
958 | 
959 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
960 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
961 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
962 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
963 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
964 |       }
965 |     }
966 | 
967 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
968 |     char quote;
969 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
970 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
971 |       quote = '\'';
972 |       doublequote = "\"";
973 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
974 |     } else {
975 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
976 |       quote = '\"';
977 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
978 |       singlequote = "\'";
979 |     }
980 | 
981 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
982 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
983 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 84-106
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
85 |     try {
86 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
87 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
88 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
89 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
90 |           logger_);
91 |     } catch (IOException e) {
92 |       compiler.report(
93 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.SCRIPT);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
106 |   }

/**
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */
Method code:
222 |     private void normalizeObjectLiteralKeyAnnotations(

226 |           value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
227 |         value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
228 |       }
229 |     }

2923 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2924 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
2925 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2926 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2927 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.

2933 |         // on var NAME = function() { ... };

2935 |       }
2936 |     }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1034-1147
Documentation:

Method code:
1034 |   private void toString(
1035 |       StringBuilder sb,
1036 |       boolean printSource,
1037 |       boolean printAnnotations,
1038 |       boolean printType) {
1039 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
1040 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
1041 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
1042 |         sb.append(' ');
1043 |         sb.append(getString());
1044 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
1045 |         sb.append(' ');
1046 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
1047 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
1048 |         // toStringTree.
1049 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
1050 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
1051 |         } else {
1052 |           sb.append(first.getString());
1053 |         }
1054 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
1055 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
1056 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
1057 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
1058 |           sb.append(' ');
1059 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
1060 |         }
1061 |         if (printSource) {
1062 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
1063 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
1064 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
1065 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
1066 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
1067 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
1068 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
1069 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
1070 |           sb.append(']');
1071 |         }
1072 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
1073 |         sb.append(' ');
1074 |         sb.append(getDouble());
1075 |       }
1076 |       if (printSource) {
1077 |         int lineno = getLineno();
1078 |         if (lineno != -1) {
1079 |           sb.append(' ');
1080 |           sb.append(lineno);
1081 |         }
1082 |       }
1083 | 
1084 |       if (printAnnotations) {
1085 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
1086 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
1087 |           int type = keys[i];
1088 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
1089 |           sb.append(" [");
1090 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
1091 |           sb.append(": ");
1092 |           String value;
1093 |           switch (type) {
1094 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
1095 |               value = "target block property";
1096 |               break;
1097 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
1098 |               value = "last local block";
1099 |               break;
1100 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
1101 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1102 |                 case BOTH:
1103 |                   value = "both";
1104 |                   break;
1105 |                 case RIGHT:
1106 |                   value = "right";
1107 |                   break;
1108 |                 case LEFT:
1109 |                   value = "left";
1110 |                   break;
1111 |                 default:
1112 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1113 |               }
1114 |               break;
1115 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1116 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1117 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1118 |                   value = "eval";
1119 |                   break;
1120 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1121 |                   value = "with";
1122 |                   break;
1123 |                 default:
1124 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1125 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1126 |               }
1127 |               break;
1128 |             default:
1129 |               value = x.toString();
1130 |               break;
1131 |           }
1132 |           sb.append(value);
1133 |           sb.append(']');
1134 |         }
1135 |       }
1136 | 
1137 |       if (printType) {
1138 |         if (jsType != null) {
1139 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1140 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1141 |             sb.append(" : ");
1142 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1143 |           }
1144 |         }
1145 |       }
1146 |     }
1147 |   }

===== M0203 =====

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 510-512
Documentation:
/**
   * Casts this to an ObjectType, or returns null if this is not an ObjectType.
   *
   * Does not change the underlying JS type. If you want to simulate JS
   * autoboxing or dereferencing, you should use autoboxesTo() or dereference().
   * Those methods may change the underlying JS type.
   */
Method code:
510 |   public ObjectType toObjectType() {
511 |     return this instanceof ObjectType ? (ObjectType) this : null;
512 |   }

===== M0204 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 234-256
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
236 | 
237 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
238 |       // which are var_args.
239 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
240 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
241 |            arg != null;
242 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
243 |         String argName = arg.getString();
244 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
245 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
246 | 
247 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
248 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
249 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
250 |         }
251 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
252 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
253 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
254 |         }
255 |       }
256 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Eats tokens until {@link JsDocToken#EOL} included, and switches back the
   * state to {@link State#SEARCHING_ANNOTATION}.
   */
Method code:
2188 |   private JsDocToken eatTokensUntilEOL(JsDocToken token) {
2189 |     do {
2190 |       if (token == JsDocToken.EOL || token == JsDocToken.EOC ||
2191 |           token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
2192 |         state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
2193 |         return token;
2194 |       }
2195 |       token = next();
2196 |     } while (true);
2197 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 456-483
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

Method code:
419 |     @Override
420 |     public Node reduce(Node node) {
421 |       if (!isReduceableFunctionExpression(node)) {
422 |         return node;
423 |       }
424 | 
425 |       Node propName = getGetPropertyName(node);
426 |       if (propName != null) {
427 |         if (propName.getType() != Token.STRING) {
428 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
429 |               "Expected STRING, got " + Token.name(propName.getType()));
430 |         }
431 | 
432 |         return buildCallNode(FACTORY_METHOD_NAME, propName,
433 |                              node.getLineno(), node.getCharno());
434 |       } else {
435 |         return node;
436 |       }
437 |     }

===== M0225 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */

193 |       }

196 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {

216 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

263 |                     }

271 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
272 |                   }

278 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;

790 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

793 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
794 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

800 | 
801 |                 case DEFINE:
802 |                 case RETURN:
803 |                 case THIS:
804 |                 case TYPE:
805 |                 case TYPEDEF:
806 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
807 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
808 | 
809 |                   Node typeNode = null;
810 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
811 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
812 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
813 |                     // it as the unknown type.
814 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
815 |                   } else {
816 |                     skipEOLs();
817 |                     token = next();
818 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
819 |                   }
820 | 
821 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
822 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
823 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
824 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
825 |                     }
826 |                   }

829 |                   if (type == null) {
830 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
831 |                     // recovering parsing

833 |                     switch (annotation) {
834 |                       case DEFINE:
835 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
836 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
837 |                               lineno, charno);
838 |                         }
839 |                         break;
840 | 
841 |                       case RETURN:
842 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
843 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
844 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
845 |                           break;
846 |                         }
847 | 
848 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
849 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
850 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
851 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
852 | 
853 |                           String returnDescription =
854 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
855 | 
856 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
857 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
858 |                                 returnDescription);
859 |                           }
860 | 
861 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
862 |                         } else {
863 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
864 |                         }
865 |                         continue retry;
866 | 
867 |                       case THIS:
868 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
869 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
870 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
871 |                         }
872 |                         break;
873 | 
874 |                       case TYPE:
875 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
876 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
877 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
878 |                         }
879 |                         break;
880 | 
881 |                       case TYPEDEF:
882 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
883 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
884 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
885 |                         }
886 |                         break;
887 |                     }
888 |                   }
889 | 
890 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
891 |                   continue retry;
892 |               }
893 |             }
894 |           }
895 |           break;
896 | 
897 |         case EOC:
898 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
899 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
900 |           }
901 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
902 |           return true;
903 | 
904 |         case EOF:
905 |           // discard any accumulated information
906 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
907 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
908 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
909 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
910 |           return false;
911 | 
912 |         case EOL:
913 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
914 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
915 |           }
916 |           token = next();
917 |           continue retry;
918 | 
919 |         default:
920 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
921 |             token = next();
922 |             continue retry;
923 |           } else {
924 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
925 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
926 |             continue retry;
927 |           }
928 |       }
929 | 
930 |       // next token
931 |       token = next();
932 |     }
933 |   }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

304 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
305 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
306 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
307 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
308 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
309 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());

313 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
314 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
315 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
316 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();

319 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);

321 |       }
322 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {

255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));

267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {

270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1988-1990
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1988 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1989 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1990 |   }

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 630-649
Documentation:

Method code:
630 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
631 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
632 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
633 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
634 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
635 |       if (typeSet == null) {
636 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
637 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
638 |       }
639 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
640 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
641 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
642 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
643 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
644 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
645 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
646 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
647 |       }
648 |     }
649 |   }

===== M0303 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 99-160
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
99 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
100 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
101 |     // just bail out early here.
102 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
103 |       return this;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
107 | 
108 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
109 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
110 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
111 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
112 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
113 |     }
114 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
115 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
116 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
117 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
118 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
119 |         }
120 |       } else {
121 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
122 |           return this;
123 |         }
124 | 
125 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
126 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
127 |         // one another.
128 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
129 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
130 |           JSType current = it.next();
131 | 
132 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
133 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
134 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
135 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
136 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
137 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
138 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
139 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
140 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
141 |               return this;
142 |             }
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
145 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
146 |               return this;
147 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
148 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
149 |               it.remove();
150 |             }
151 |           }
152 |         }
153 |         alternates.add(alternate);
154 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       result = null;
158 |     }
159 |     return this;
160 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTokenType(int)

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0329 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 228-291
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
228 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
229 |     this.options = options;
230 |     if (errorManager == null) {
231 |       if (outStream == null) {
232 |         setErrorManager(
233 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
234 |       } else {
235 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
236 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
237 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
238 |         setErrorManager(printer);
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
243 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
244 |       options.checkTypes = true;
245 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
246 |       options.checkTypes = false;
247 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
248 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
249 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
250 |       // parser type warnings.
251 |       options.setWarningLevel(
252 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
253 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
254 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
258 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
259 |       options.setWarningLevel(
260 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
261 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
265 |       options.setWarningLevel(
266 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
267 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
268 |     }
269 | 
270 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
271 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
272 |     guards.add(
273 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
274 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
275 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
276 | 
277 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
278 | 
279 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
280 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
281 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
282 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
283 |     // error off.
284 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
285 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
286 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
287 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
288 |     }
289 | 
290 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
291 |   }

155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
156 |     switch (n.getType()) {
157 |       // Childless expressions
158 |       case Token.FALSE:
159 |       case Token.NULL:
160 |       case Token.THIS:
161 |       case Token.TRUE:

163 |         return;
164 | 
165 |       // General uniary ops
166 |       case Token.DELPROP:
167 |       case Token.POS:
168 |       case Token.NEG:
169 |       case Token.NOT:
170 |       case Token.INC:
171 |       case Token.DEC:
172 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
173 |       case Token.VOID:
174 |       case Token.BITNOT:
175 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
176 |         return;
177 | 
178 |       // General binary ops
179 |       case Token.COMMA:
180 |       case Token.OR:
181 |       case Token.AND:
182 |       case Token.BITOR:
183 |       case Token.BITXOR:
184 |       case Token.BITAND:
185 |       case Token.EQ:
186 |       case Token.NE:

188 |       case Token.SHNE:
189 |       case Token.LT:
190 |       case Token.GT:
191 |       case Token.LE:
192 |       case Token.GE:
193 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
194 |       case Token.IN:
195 |       case Token.LSH:
196 |       case Token.RSH:
197 |       case Token.URSH:
198 |       case Token.SUB:
199 |       case Token.ADD:
200 |       case Token.MUL:
201 |       case Token.MOD:
202 |       case Token.DIV:
203 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       // Assignments
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
218 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
219 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
220 |         return;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.HOOK:
223 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
224 |         return;
225 | 
226 |       // Node types that require special handling
227 |       case Token.STRING:
228 |         validateString(n);
229 |         return;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.NUMBER:
232 |         validateNumber(n);
233 |         return;
234 | 
235 |       case Token.NAME:
236 |         validateName(n);
237 |         return;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.GETELEM:
240 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
241 |         return;
242 | 
243 |       case Token.GETPROP:
244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.REGEXP:
256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.CALL:
260 |         validateCall(n);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.NEW:
264 |         validateNew(n);
265 |         return;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
268 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
269 |         return;
270 | 
271 |       default:

===== M0350 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 435-483
Documentation:

Method code:
435 |   @Override
436 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
437 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
438 |       return true;
439 |     }
440 | 
441 |     // Union types
442 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
443 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
444 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
445 |       return false;
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     // record types
449 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
450 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
451 |     }
452 | 
453 |     // Interfaces
454 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
455 |     // to the interface instance.
456 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
457 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
458 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
459 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
460 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
461 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
462 |           return true;
463 |         }
464 |       }
465 |     }
466 | 
467 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     // other prototype based objects
476 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
477 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
478 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
479 |       // to avoid guessing.
480 |       return true;
481 |     }
482 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
483 |   }

736 |             "identifier is a reserved word",
737 |             sourceName,
738 |             nameNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
739 |         }
740 |         return newStringNode(Token.NAME, nameNode.getIdentifier());
741 |       }
742 |     }
