===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1332-1340
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1332 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1333 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1334 |       return -1;
1335 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1336 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1337 |     } else {
1338 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
2077 |   public Node cloneTree() {
2078 |     Node result = cloneNode();
2079 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
2080 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
2081 |       n2clone.parent = result;
2082 |       if (result.last != null) {

2087 |       }

2089 |     }
2090 |     return result;
2091 |   }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0379 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 486-506
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
486 |   @Override
487 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
488 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
489 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
490 |     if (unknown) {
491 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
492 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
493 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
494 |         unknown = false;
495 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
496 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
497 |             unknown = true;
498 |             break;
499 |           }
500 |         }
501 |       } else {
502 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return unknown;
506 |   }

===== M0397 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 167-188
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
167 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
168 |     if (isAllType) {
169 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
170 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
171 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
172 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
173 |       } else {
174 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
175 |       }
176 |     } else {
177 |       int size = alternates.size();
178 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
179 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
180 |       } else if (size > 1) {
181 |         return null;
182 |       } else if (size == 1) {
183 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
184 |       } else {
185 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
186 |       }
187 |     }
188 |   }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 735-759
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
735 |   @Override
736 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
737 |     FunctionType that =
738 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType.toMaybeFunctionType());
739 |     if (that == null) {
740 |       return false;
741 |     }
742 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
743 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
744 |         return this == that;
745 |       }
746 |       return false;
747 |     }
748 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
749 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
750 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
751 |       }
752 |       return false;
753 |     }
754 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
755 |       return false;
756 |     }
757 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
758 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
759 |   }

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.

   *     normal jsdoc, or no jsdoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).

336 |   private JsDocInfoParser createJsDocInfoParser(Comment node, Node irNode) {

===== M0418 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 466-475
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
466 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
467 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
468 |          current != null;
469 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
470 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
471 |         return true;
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 |     return false;
475 |   }

===== M0426 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 944-971
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
944 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
945 |     // unknown
946 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
947 |       return true;
948 |     }
949 |     // equality
950 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
951 |       return true;
952 |     }
953 |     // all type
954 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
955 |       return true;
956 |     }
957 |     // unions
958 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
959 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
960 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
961 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
962 |           return true;
963 |         }
964 |       }
965 |     }
966 |     // named types
967 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
968 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
969 |     }
970 |     return false;
971 |   }

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionRewriter.SetterReducer.getSetPropertyName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionRewriter.java
Original range: 508-536
Documentation:
/**
     * Checks if the function matches the pattern:
     *   function(<value>, <rest>) {this.<name> = <value>}
     * and returns <name> if a match is found.
     *
     * @return STRING node that is the rhs of a this property get; or null.
     */
Method code:
508 |     private Node getSetPropertyName(Node functionNode) {
509 |       Node body = functionNode.getLastChild();
510 |       if (!body.hasOneChild()) {
511 |         return null;
512 |       }
513 | 
514 |       Node argList = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
515 |       Node paramNode = argList.getFirstChild();
516 |       if (paramNode == null) {
517 |         return null;
518 |       }
519 | 
520 |       Node statement = body.getFirstChild();
521 |       if (!NodeUtil.isExprAssign(statement)) {
522 |         return null;
523 |       }
524 | 
525 |       Node assign = statement.getFirstChild();
526 |       Node lhs = assign.getFirstChild();
527 |       if (NodeUtil.isGetProp(lhs) && NodeUtil.isThis(lhs.getFirstChild())) {
528 |         Node rhs = assign.getLastChild();
529 |         if (NodeUtil.isName(rhs) &&
530 |             rhs.getString().equals(paramNode.getString())) {
531 |           Node propertyName = lhs.getLastChild();
532 |           return propertyName;
533 |         }
534 |       }
535 |       return null;
536 |     }

118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {

127 |       case Token.EQ:

131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {

207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));

217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:

277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:

280 |     }

===== M0464 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1005-1075
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1005 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
1006 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
1007 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
1008 |                           String backslashEscape,
1009 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
1010 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1011 |     sb.append(quote);
1012 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1013 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1014 |       switch (c) {
1015 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1016 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1017 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1018 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1019 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1020 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1021 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1022 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1023 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1024 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1025 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1026 |             sb.append("\\>");
1027 |           } else {
1028 |             sb.append(c);
1029 |           }
1030 |           break;
1031 |         case '<':
1032 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1033 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1034 | 
1035 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1036 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1037 | 
1038 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1039 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1040 |             sb.append("<\\");
1041 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1042 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1043 |             sb.append("<\\");
1044 |           } else {
1045 |             sb.append(c);
1046 |           }
1047 |           break;
1048 |         default:
1049 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1050 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1051 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1052 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1053 |               sb.append(c);
1054 |             } else {
1055 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1056 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1057 |             }
1058 |           } else {
1059 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1060 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1061 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1062 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1063 |               sb.append(c);
1064 |             } else {
1065 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1066 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1067 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1068 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1069 |             }
1070 |           }
1071 |       }
1072 |     }
1073 |     sb.append(quote);
1074 |     return sb.toString();
1075 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.

   *     length argument.

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Original range: 616-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
616 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
617 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
618 |     if (typeSet == null) {
619 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
620 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
624 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
625 | 
626 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
627 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
628 |   }

===== M0518 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1151-1289
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1151 |   Node parseInputs() {
1152 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1153 | 
1154 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1155 |     // individual file parse trees.
1156 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1157 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1158 |     }
1159 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1160 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1161 |     }
1162 | 
1163 |     // Parse main js sources.
1164 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1165 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1166 | 
1167 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1168 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1169 | 
1170 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1171 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1172 | 
1173 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1174 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1175 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1176 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1177 |     }
1178 | 
1179 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1180 | 
1181 |     try {
1182 |       // Parse externs sources.
1183 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1184 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1185 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1186 |           return null;
1187 |         }
1188 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1189 |       }
1190 | 
1191 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1192 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1193 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1194 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1195 | 
1196 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1197 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1198 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1199 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1200 |           }
1201 |         }
1202 | 
1203 |         try {
1204 |           inputs =
1205 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1206 |               .manageDependencies(
1207 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1208 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1209 |           report(JSError.make(
1210 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1211 |           return null;
1212 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1213 |           report(JSError.make(
1214 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1215 |           return null;
1216 |         }
1217 |       }
1218 | 
1219 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1220 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1221 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1222 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1223 | 
1224 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1225 |         if (n == null) {
1226 |           continue;
1227 |         }
1228 | 
1229 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1230 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1231 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1232 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1233 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1234 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1235 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1236 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1237 | 
1238 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1239 | 
1240 |             externs.add(input);
1241 |             staleInputs = true;
1242 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1243 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1244 |             staleInputs = true;
1245 |           }
1246 |         }
1247 |       }
1248 | 
1249 |       if (staleInputs) {
1250 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1251 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1252 |       }
1253 | 
1254 |       // Build the AST.
1255 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1256 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1257 |         if (n == null) {
1258 |           continue;
1259 |         }
1260 | 
1261 |         if (devMode) {
1262 |           runSanityCheck();
1263 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1264 |             return null;
1265 |           }
1266 |         }
1267 | 
1268 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1269 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1270 | 
1271 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1272 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1273 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1274 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1275 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1276 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1277 |         }
1278 | 
1279 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1280 |       }
1281 | 
1282 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1283 |         return null;
1284 |       }
1285 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1286 |     } finally {
1287 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1288 |     }
1289 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverse(Node)

/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively.
   */
Method code:
253 |   public void traverse(Node root) {
254 |     try {

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 430-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
430 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
431 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
432 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
433 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
434 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
435 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
436 |         String inputName = input.getName();
437 | 
438 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
439 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
440 |         // will get an error down the line.
441 |         inputs.add(input);
442 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
443 |       }
444 |     }
445 |     return inputs;
446 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 485-508
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
485 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
486 |     switch (n.getType()) {
487 |       case Token.STRING:
488 |       case Token.NUMBER:
489 |       case Token.NULL:
490 |       case Token.TRUE:
491 |       case Token.FALSE:
492 |         return true;
493 |       case Token.NOT:
494 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
495 |       case Token.VOID:
496 |       case Token.NEG:
497 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
498 |       case Token.NAME:
499 |         String name = n.getString();
500 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
501 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
502 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
503 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
504 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     return false;
508 |   }

===== M0558 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getSortedPropTypes()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 961-975
Documentation:
// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
961 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
962 |     int count = 0;
963 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
964 |       count++;
965 |     }
966 | 
967 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
968 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
969 |       count--;
970 |       keys[count] = x.getType();
971 |     }
972 | 
973 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
974 |     return keys;
975 |   }

===== M0565 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 398-411
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
398 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
399 |       Node ret;
400 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
401 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
402 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
403 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
404 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
405 |       } else {
406 |         ret = transform(n);
407 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
408 |       }
409 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
410 |       return ret;
411 |     }

===== M0571 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionRewriter.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionRewriter.java
Original range: 56-103
Documentation:

Method code:
56 |   @Override
57 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
58 |     List<Reducer> reducers = ImmutableList.of(new ReturnConstantReducer(),
59 |                                               new GetterReducer(),
60 |                                               new SetterReducer(),
61 |                                               new EmptyFunctionReducer(),
62 |                                               new IdentityReducer());
63 | 
64 |     Multimap<Reducer, Reduction> reductionMap = HashMultimap.create();
65 | 
66 |     // Accumulate possible reductions in the reduction multi map.  They
67 |     // will be applied in the loop below.
68 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
69 |                            new ReductionGatherer(reducers, reductionMap));
70 | 
71 |     // Apply reductions iff they will provide some savings.
72 |     for (Reducer reducer : reducers) {
73 |       Collection<Reduction> reductions = reductionMap.get(reducer);
74 |       if (reductions.isEmpty()) {
75 |         continue;
76 |       }
77 | 
78 |       Node helperCode = parseHelperCode(reducer);
79 |       if (helperCode == null) {
80 |         continue;
81 |       }
82 | 
83 |       int helperCodeCost = InlineCostEstimator.getCost(helperCode);
84 | 
85 |       // Estimate savings
86 |       int savings = 0;
87 |       for (Reduction reduction : reductions) {
88 |         savings += reduction.estimateSavings();
89 |       }
90 | 
91 |       // Compare estimated savings against the helper cost.  Apply
92 |       // reductions if doing so will result in some savings.
93 |       if (savings > (helperCodeCost + SAVINGS_THRESHOLD)) {
94 |         for (Reduction reduction : reductions) {
95 |           reduction.apply();
96 |         }
97 | 
98 |         Node addingRoot = compiler.getNodeForCodeInsertion(null);
99 |         addingRoot.addChildrenToFront(helperCode);
100 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
101 |       }
102 |     }
103 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 761-798
Documentation:

Method code:
761 |     @Override
762 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
763 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
764 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
765 |       }
766 | 
767 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
768 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
769 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
770 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
771 |             reportGetter(el);
772 |             continue;
773 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
774 |             reportSetter(el);
775 |             continue;
776 |           }
777 |         }
778 | 
779 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
780 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
781 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
782 |           key.setType(Token.GET);
783 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
784 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
785 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
786 |           }
787 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
788 |           key.setType(Token.SET);
789 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
790 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
791 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
792 |           }
793 |         }
794 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
795 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
796 |       }
797 |       return node;
798 |     }

55 |   @Override
56 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
57 |     if (checkOnly) {
58 |       normalizeNodeTypes(root);
59 |     } else {
60 |       // Don't perform "PrepareAnnotations" when doing checks as
61 |       // they currently aren't valid during sanity checks.  In particular,
62 |       // they DIRECT_EVAL shouldn't be applied after inlining has been
63 |       // performed.
64 |       if (externs != null) {
65 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
66 |             compiler, externs, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
67 |       }
68 |       if (root != null) {
69 |         NodeTraversal.traverse(
70 |             compiler, root, new PrepareAnnotations(compiler));
71 |       }
72 |     }
73 |   }

711 |     }

713 |     last = child;

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1161-1233
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1181 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1182 |       case Token.EQ:
1183 |       case Token.NE:
1184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1185 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1186 |       case Token.LT:
1187 |       case Token.GT:
1188 |       case Token.LE:
1189 |       case Token.GE:
1190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1191 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1192 |       case Token.LSH:
1193 |       case Token.RSH:
1194 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1195 |       case Token.SUB:
1196 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1197 |       case Token.MUL:
1198 |       case Token.MOD:
1199 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1200 |       case Token.INC:
1201 |       case Token.DEC:
1202 |       case Token.NEW:
1203 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1204 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1205 |       case Token.VOID:
1206 |       case Token.NOT:
1207 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.CALL:
1212 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1213 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1214 |       // Data values
1215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1216 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1217 |       case Token.FALSE:
1218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1219 |       case Token.NAME:
1220 |       case Token.NULL:
1221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1222 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1223 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1224 |       case Token.STRING:
1225 |       case Token.THIS:
1226 |       case Token.TRUE:
1227 |         return 15;
1228 | 
1229 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1230 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1231 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1232 |     }
1233 |   }

===== M0588 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 331-364
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
331 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
332 |     if (prototype == null) {
333 |       return false;
334 |     }
335 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
336 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
337 |       return false;
338 |     }
339 | 
340 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
341 |     this.prototype = prototype;
342 |     this.prototypeSlot = new SimpleSlot("prototype", prototype, true);
343 | 
344 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
345 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
346 |       if (superClass != null) {
347 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       if (isInterface()) {
351 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
352 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
353 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
354 |           }
355 |         }
356 |       }
357 |     }
358 | 
359 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
360 |       clearCachedValues();
361 |     }
362 | 
363 |     return true;
364 |   }

===== M0591 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 486-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
486 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
487 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
488 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
489 | 
490 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
491 | 
492 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
493 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
494 | 
495 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
496 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
497 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
498 |     }
499 | 
500 |     curNode = n;
501 |     pushScope(n);
502 | 
503 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
504 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
505 |       // scope.
506 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
510 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
511 | 
512 |     // Args
513 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
514 | 
515 |     // Body
516 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
517 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
518 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
519 | 
520 |     popScope();
521 |   }

===== M0599 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 877-890
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
877 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
878 |     if (item == null) {
879 |       return null;
880 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
881 |       return item.getNext();
882 |     } else {
883 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
884 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
885 |         return item.chain(result);
886 |       } else {
887 |         return item;
888 |       }
889 |     }
890 |   }
